### [1] [Scalar-scalar-tensor interaction in DHOST theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20730)
*S. Mironov,V. Volkova*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了二次退化高阶标量-张量（DHOST）理论中宇宙学背景上扰动的一般三次作用量，聚焦于标量-标量-张量相互作用，并采用完全协变形式及扰动层面的幺正规范，为估算晚期宇宙暗能量模型中引力波衰变为两个标量激发的速率提供关键输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 估算晚期宇宙中引力波衰变为两个标量激发的衰变速率，以检验二次DHOST暗能量模型的可观测效应。

Method: 在二次DHOST理论的标量场扰动背景下，采用完全协变表述并施加幺正规范，系统推导包含标量-标量-张量耦合的三阶扰动作用量。

Result: 得到一般性的三阶作用量表达式，明确刻画了标量-标量-张量顶点结构及其与DHOST系数的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 该三次作用量是计算GW→φφ衰变过程散射振幅的基础，为未来引力波探测器检验DHOST理论提供了可证伪的唯象预言。

Abstract: We derive the general cubic action for perturbations about a cosmological background in the quadratic subclass of degenerate higher-order scalar-tensor (DHOST) theories, focusing on scalar-scalar-tensor interactions. We adopt a fully covariant formulation and implement unitary gauge at the level of perturbations. This mixed sector provides the key ingredient for estimating the decay rate of a gravitational wave into two scalar excitations in the quadratic DHOST models of dark energy in the late-time Universe.

</details>


### [2] [Study of Cosmic Acceleration of the Universe in the Presence of Bulk Viscous Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20759)
*Dheeraj Singh Rana*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究基于非黎曼几何的引力理论，特别是含体粘度的f(Q)引力模型，通过精确宇宙学解和观测约束，验证其对宇宙晚期加速膨胀的解释能力，并分析其在不同宇宙演化阶段的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义相对论虽成功，但难以自然解释宇宙加速膨胀等观测现象，促使人们探索基于非黎曼几何（如仿射无挠曲率Q）的替代理论，尤其结合流体粘性以改善晚期宇宙动力学行为。

Method: 构建含体粘度的f(Q)引力模型，求解各向同性均匀宇宙中的精确弗里德曼方程；利用Ia型超新星、哈勃参数、重子声波振荡等观测数据对模型参数进行贝叶斯约束；结合动力系统分析与状态方程演化，检验相空间稳定性及宇宙学历史再现能力。

Result: 获得若干解析可解的粘性f(Q)宇宙学模型，参数经观测约束后能复现z<2.5范围内的H(z)与距离模数；动力系统分析表明部分模型存在稳定加速吸引子，且可实现辐射→物质主导→暗能量主导的三阶段演化。

Conclusion: 含体粘度的f(Q)引力是一类具有良好宇宙学可行性的修正引力理论，其无需引入标量场或暗能量成分即可产生有效晚期加速，且参数空间与当前观测兼容，为检验引力几何本质提供了新路径。

Abstract: Over the past century, both theoretical advancements and experimental observations have established General Relativity (GR) as the most successful framework for describing gravitational phenomena. However, observations from multiple cosmological probes over the last two decades have provided compelling evidence for the accelerated expansion of the Universe. The rapid progress in observational astronomy and precision cosmology has highlighted several challenges that motivate the search for extensions or alternatives to General Relativity. In this thesis, we investigate alternative formulations of gravity based on non-Riemannian geometry, with particular emphasis on (f(Q)) gravity in the presence of bulk viscosity. We first explore the existence of exact cosmological solutions for viscous fluid models within the framework of (f(Q)) gravity and constrain the free parameters of these solutions using observational datasets. The resulting constrained models are then employed to study the evolutionary history of cosmic expansion. Our analysis demonstrates that the proposed viscous (f(Q)) gravity models can successfully account for the observed late-time acceleration of the Universe. Furthermore, we examine several classes of nonlinear viscous (f(Q)) gravity models through both dynamical systems and observational analyses. Particular attention is given to the cosmological viability of these models and their ability to reproduce the different evolutionary epochs of the Universe, ranging from matter-dominated eras to accelerated expansion phases.

</details>


### [3] [Selective enhancement of quantum decay channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20766)
*Pritam Nanda,Kinjalk Lochan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在腔体环境中如何通过调控边界条件增强量子粒子的低能衰变率，指出腔体几何结构可显著提升红外区域的模式密度和跃迁概率，从而替代高能实验来研究QED及新物理过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在自由空间中，低能衰变受限于红外区域模式密度的匮乏；为增强衰变率，需依赖高能区的跃迁概率与模式密度协同作用。本文旨在探索如何在可控边界条件下（如光学/微波腔）突破该限制。

Method: 基于量子场论框架，分析衰变率作为模式密度与跃迁概率密度卷积的物理结构；比较自由空间与腔体中模式函数的红外行为；结合共振腔几何参数，定量评估其对低能衰变通道的增强效应。

Result: 证明腔体环境可显著增强红外模式函数的支持度，导致低能衰变概率与速率大幅提升；识别出若干几何共振‘甜点’，使主场与产物场相互作用强度剧增。

Conclusion: 腔体几何设计可作为有效调控低能量子衰变过程的工具，为QED精密测量及新物理探针提供无需高能加速器的替代路径。

Abstract: In the decay of quantum particles under field theoretic consideration, the decay rate is typically a convolution of the density of modes the primary field is allowed to decay into and the allowed probability density for the field to decay into such modes. In free space, though many such processes show high amplitude of such transitions towards the infrared sector, the depletion of allowed mode density in that regime arrests the efficacy of such decays at low energies. Therefore in free space, in order to enhance the decay rate, one needs the transition probability density to be rich enough towards the high energy sector where mode density support is also high enough to make the rate sufficiently large. In this work we argue that in the controlled boundary condition environment e.g. in a cavity, the mode functions of product field receive significant support towards their infrared sector, boosting the probability (and hence rates) of low energy processes. The cavity geometry offers sweet spots in terms of resonant geometry around which the interaction of a primary field with product fields receives dramatic enhancement, significantly enlarging its decay rates. Therefore, a judicious selection of cavity geometry serves as a potential substitute to studying interesting processes at high energy. The results have direct relevance for the study of QED processes and implications for the study of exotic new physics are also discussed.

</details>


### [4] [The Cosmological Constant Problem: An Accessible Introduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20778)
*Ali Kaya,Adam Lahey*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文以一个实标量场为例，系统讲解了宇宙学常数问题，重点展示了如何在平直时空和膨胀宇宙中计算量子真空能密度与压强，并比较了维数正规化、截断正规化和绝热正规化方法；指出120个数量级的理论-观测差异源于未考虑正规化与重整化的粗略估计，但即使正确处理，QFT仍导致巨大不匹配，并强调宇宙膨胀背景下的概念难题（如共动/物理尺度选择、真空态非唯一性）


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清宇宙学常数问题中常被误解的120阶不匹配来源，同时揭示即使在规范处理下仍存在的根本性理论张力，并阐明膨胀宇宙中真空定义与能量诠释的概念困难

Method: 采用一个质量实标量场作为玩具模型，在经典FRW背景上计算其量子真空期望值；系统应用维度正规化、硬截断正规化与绝热正规化三种技术，并对比其对真空能量-动量张量分量的影响；区分共动坐标与物理坐标下的紫外截断选择，分析其对压强和能量密度标度行为的影响

Result: 证实正规化方式显著影响真空能量密度的数值与发散结构，但无法消除其与观测宇宙学常数之间的巨大数量级差异；揭示在膨胀时空中真空态依赖于时间切片与哈密顿量选择，导致真空能量非协变、非唯一；指出‘真空能量驱动宇宙加速’这一图景在QFT框架内缺乏协变、可重整的微观基础

Conclusion: 宇宙学常数问题本质上是量子场论与广义相对论在背景几何动力学上的深层不相容：真空能量在弯曲时空中的定义、正规化与重整化均面临根本性概念障碍，单纯改进正规化方案无法解决该问题，需超越标准QFT+GR范式的新物理。

Abstract: We present a pedagogical introduction to the cosmological constant problem that requires only basic knowledge of quantum field theory and general relativity. A massive real scalar field is used to illustrate how the quantum vacuum energy density and pressure can be calculated both in flat spacetime and in an expanding universe. Detailed computations are provided for dimensional, cutoff, and adiabatic regularizations. No attempt is made to address quantum gravitational effects, and the expanding-universe background is treated classically. We point out that although the commonly cited discrepancy of 120 orders of magnitude between theory and observation is based on an estimate that does not account for regularization and renormalization, fundamental principles of quantum field theory nevertheless lead to a huge mismatch. In addition to this large discrepancy, we emphasize that there are also conceptual challenges related to cosmic expansion, such as the choice between comoving and physical scales in certain contexts and the non-uniqueness of vacuum.

</details>


### [5] [$\mathrm{ISIM}(2)$ gravitational gauge model via Soft Algebra formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20806)
*J. M. B. Matzenbacher,J. M. Hoff da Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文基于软代数形式主义，通过对ISIM(2)洛伦兹子群参数的规范化，构建了一个无挠率的引力模型，并给出了其不变作用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建基于ISIM(2)对称性的引力理论，拓展标准广义相对论框架下的对称性结构。

Method: 采用软代数（soft algebra）形式主义对sim(2)李代数及其非齐次扩展进行参数化，并在此基础上构造无挠率（torsionless）的联络与曲率，进而导出协变作用量。

Result: 得到了一个基于ISIM(2)规范对称性的、完全协变且无挠率的引力模型，并给出了其闭合形式的作用量表达式。

Conclusion: ISIM(2)作为低维或渐近平坦时空中的重要对称性，其规范理论可为研究引力波、BMS拓展及渐近对称性提供新路径；该模型为理解平直时空边界上的引力动力学提供了有效场论框架。

Abstract: This work presents a comprehensive account of a gravitational model based on the gauging of the $\mathrm{ISIM}(2)$ Lorentz subgroup parameters through the so-called soft algebra formalism. After presenting a closed form for $\mathfrak{sim}(2)$ and its inhomogeneous extension, we proceed to develop a complete torsionless gravitational model, culminating in an invariant action.

</details>


### [6] [Exact solutions using power law scalar potential in the Saez-Ballester-K-essence like theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20894)
*J. Socorro,A. Gil-Ocaranza,Ximena López-Mujica,Cesar Aarón Pacheco-Vázquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了一类基于负幂律Sáez-Ballester势的K-essence宇宙学模型，通过场重定义将动力学映射到已解FLRW模型，获得尺度因子与标量场的精确经典解；其经典宇宙学演化呈现趋向de Sitter的晚期加速膨胀（q → −1）；进而导出Wheeler-DeWitt方程并求得精确量子解，实现了该K-essence类模型的一致经典-量子描述。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有可解性与物理合理性的K-essence模型，以统一描述晚期宇宙加速膨胀，并建立其经典与量子层面的自洽理论框架。

Method: 采用场重定义φ → φ将原K-essence系统映射至已知可解的FLRW模型；在Hamiltonian形式下求解经典运动方程；推导Wheeler-DeWitt方程并构造其精确量子解。

Result: 获得尺度因子a(t)与标量场φ(t)的显式经典解；证明晚期q → −1的de Sitter型加速；得到Wheeler-DeWitt方程的精确量子态解，标量场在量子层面表现为宇宙演化的背景结构。

Conclusion: 该负幂律Sáez-Ballester型K-essence模型不仅支持观测相符的晚期加速，且具备完全可解性，其经典与量子描述高度自洽，为探究暗能量本质及量子宇宙学提供了严格可处理的范例。

Abstract: We investigate a K-essence like cosmological model whose scalar-field potential is constructed from a negative power-law Sáez--Ballester potential. By means of a suitable field redefinition from $φ$ to $\varphi$, we show that the resulting field equations acquire a mathematical structure analogous to that of a previously solved Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker (FLRW) cosmological model. This correspondence allows us to obtain exact classical solutions for both the scale factor and the scalar field within the Hamiltonian formalism. The resulting cosmological dynamics exhibits a late-time accelerated expansion, with the deceleration parameter approaching the asymptotic value $q\rightarrow -1$, characteristic of a de Sitter phase. At the quantum level, the corresponding Wheeler-DeWitt (WDW) equation is derived and exact quantum solutions are obtained. These results provide a consistent classical and quantum description of the cosmological evolution generated by this class of K-essence models. In this formalism, the scalar field remains as a cosmic background where the universe unfolds, which is glimpsed from the quantum solution perspective.

</details>


### [7] [Scattering, Hawking Radiation and Neutrino Energy Deposition in Euler-Heisenberg Black Holes Surrounded by Perfect Fluid Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20931)
*Ramon Becar,P. A. Gonzalez,Ali Ovgun,Joel Saavedra,Yerko Vasquez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了受完美流体暗物质（PFDM）包围的Euler-Heisenberg（EH）黑洞的动力学与散射性质，计算了标量、电磁及有效轴向自旋-2扰动的准正规模谱、灰体因子、吸收截面、霍金辐射谱，并分析了中微子-反中微子湮灭通道的相对论增强效应；结果表明PFDM参数收缩光学结构、增强阻尼率并抑制透射，而EH修正仅在电荷足够大时才显著影响近视界几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非线性电动力学（Euler-Heisenberg理论）与暗物质环境（完美流体暗物质晕）对黑洞散射与稳定性的影响，建立统一框架以区分二者在可观测量（如QNM、灰体因子、辐射谱）中的特征性贡献。

Method: 在固定EH+PFDM背景度规上求解标量场、电磁场及构造的有效轴向自旋-2扰动方程；采用13阶WKB方法结合Padé重求和计算准正规模；直接数值积分径向波动方程获得精确灰体因子；解析推导近极值极限下的纯虚频分支；计算吸收截面与霍金谱；评估νν̄湮灭的相对论增强。

Result: PFDM参数使光球角频率增大、Lyapunov指数升高，导致QNM振荡频率上升、阻尼率增强、光学半径收缩、透射抑制；EH耦合仅在大电荷下显著修正近视界几何；发现近极值下存在纯虚QNM分支，其阻尼率随PFDM参数单调增加且近似与自旋无关；灰体因子低于解析下界；νν̄湮灭功率密度受PFDM增强。

Conclusion: PFDM主导散射与稳定性行为，其效应远强于EH修正（除非电荷极大）；该工作为通过引力波与电磁探针联合甄别暗物质晕与非线性电动力学提供了可检验的理论基准。

Abstract: We study the dynamical and scattering properties for the Euler-Heisenberg BH surrounded by perfect fluid dark matter. The geometry contains a compact non-linear electrodynamic correction governed by the EH coupling and a logarithmic dark-matter contribution governed by the surrounding PFDM halo. We study the scalar, electromagnetic and a particular effective axial spin-2 channel constructed on the fixed EH plus PFDM background, acting as a proxy for the gravitational-like perturbation problem and not for the fully coupled gravitational perturbation problem. We compute the quasinormal-mode spectrum employing a thirteenth-order WKB method supplemented with Padé resummation and compare it with the eikonal prediction calculated in terms of the angular frequency of the photon sphere and the Lyapunov exponent. Moreover, we study the near-extremal configurations and derive a purely imaginary branch of quasinormal frequencies in the near-horizon region, whose damping rate increases with the PFDM parameter and is nearly spin-independent. We then compute exact greybody factors by direct numerical integration of the radial wave equation and compare them to analytical lower bounds. We also analyze the absorption cross sections and the Hawking emission spectra. We also calculate the relativistic enhancement of the neutrino-antineutrino annihilation channel outside the EHPFDM black hole. We find that the PFDM parameter contracts the optical structure, increases the oscillation frequency, enhances the damping rate and suppresses transmission. On the other hand, the Euler-Heisenberg correction leads to a weaker near-horizon deformation whose effect becomes relevant for sufficiently large charge. These results provide a common scattering framework for comparing the impact of dark-matter environments and nonlinear electrodynamics

</details>


### [8] [Quantum Dust from the Curse of Dimensionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20943)
*Kenan Oggad*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨量子引力理论中普遍出现的短程时空二维现象，指出这是一种由Fubini-Study流形上纯态空间的浓度现象导致的测量假象，而非真实几何维度；其核心判据是Laplacian谱在零附近的渐近密度，作者用Lean 4形式化验证了该机制，并提出‘谱保真性’作为区分真实维度与测量伪影的关键条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何多种不相关的量子引力理论（如LQG、Causal Sets、String Theory等）均在普朗克尺度下给出有效谱维数为2，而该现象是否反映真实时空结构尚无统一理解。

Method: 基于纯态空间的Fubini-Study几何，利用测度集中现象导出随机态样本的等距坍缩；引入扩散探针定义运行谱维数；通过Laplacian算子小特征值分布（Beta律、幂律尾、能隙结构）分类维度读数；全部关键命题在Lean 4 + Mathlib中形式化验证。

Result: 证明谱维数d_s=2在大样本极限下是Fubini-Study空间上通用的测量伪影，其起源是单松弛尺度下的等距尘埃结构；真实维度需由Laplacian谱在零附近的本征值密度决定——幂律ρ(λ)∼λ^{(d/2)−1}对应真实d维，单能隙上方单尺度对应人工2维，双尺度能带对应普适线性行为；提出‘谱保真性’作为 emergence map 下维度可迁移性的充要条件。

Conclusion: d_s=2并非量子引力的深层结构信号，而是高维希尔伯特空间上几何测量的普遍退相干效应；判定某理论是否真正实现2维时空，须检验其物理非局域算子的Laplacian谱是否满足谱保真性——即小本征值分布能否忠实映射至低能有效几何。

Abstract: Why do unrelated approaches to quantum gravity nearly all find spacetime two-dimensional at the shortest scales? Each theory answers only within its own dynamics; we highlight a single kinematic route to the same value, one assuming no field equation and living in the geometry of the space of states alone. That route is concentration of measure on the Fubini-Study geometry of pure states, which forces the pairwise distances of a random sample to equalize as the dimension grows, so any finite sample collapses to an equidistant dust whose thresholded metric graph is the complete graph. Handed this dust, a diffusion probe reads it as two-dimensional in the large-sample limit, the value the running spectral dimension takes at the dust's single relaxation scale, a property of the measurement rather than the structure; this convergence on two is not, by itself, evidence that spacetime is two-dimensional. Whether a given two is such an artifact is governed by the Laplacian spectrum near zero, and whether that reading carries across an emergence map is the condition we call spectral faithfulness; a single relaxation scale encodes no spectral dimension that tells one structure from another. The collapse, the probe value, and the eigenvalue-density criterion are machine-checked in Lean 4 against Mathlib, resting on the standard Beta law of overlaps; a power-law tail of small eigenvalues reads a genuine dimension, a single scale above a gap reads two at its own clock, and a gapped two-scale band reads off the universal line. These classes are run on graph-Laplacian proxies, and whether a link-graph reading carries to the physical nonlocal operator is left open. The spectral test reads the eigenvalue density near zero and separates, on a given structure, a measurement artifact from a dimension the structure genuinely expresses.

</details>


### [9] [Out-of-Equilibrium Effects in Non-Radial Relativistic Stellar Perturbations: A Model-Agnostic Formulation and Mode Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20957)
*Takuya Katagiri,Victor Guedes,Kent Yagi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个模型无关的、通用的相对论恒星非径向振荡线性扰动框架，首次系统纳入了非平衡效应（如粘滞和热导率）对完美流体部分的修正，并应用于Bemfica-Disconzi-Noronha-Kovtun流体，研究其对振荡模频率与阻尼时间的影响。

In addition, the fate of the Universe is studied through late-time singularities, namely the big, little, and pseudo-rip, within the Granda--Oliveros FHDE setting. In short, this dissertation proposes FHDE as a theoretically motivated extension of HDE, bridging non-locality in quantum gravity with the late-time dynamics of the Universe, and offering a route toward understanding cosmic acceleration beyond \(Λ\)CDM.

The mechanism is photon-rocket recoil on a tidally protected, exactly flat cavity; the warp drive is the decoupled flat interior, not a warp field. The exterior energy conditions reduce to $n^2\ge0$, and steering obeys $-\dot m\ge 3m|a|$, paid for by Bondi mass loss. The shell satisfies the surface dominant energy condition for $2m/R<24/25$; admissible accelerating shells exist near the Schwarzschild--Minkowski anchor and, for slow burns, as time-evolved spacetimes. On the gravitational-wave--silent class, the optimal maneuver is the Damour dipole. The wall is marginally stable, with strict stability available at no energy-condition cost; fully dynamical flux-coupled stability remains open. The drive is causal, subluminal, and energetically costly but positive-energy: steering is a problem of energy budget, not exotic matter.

Abstract: We present the first study of Leggett-Garg inequality violation in polarized muon spin precession. This allows the resummation of large logarithms to N$^3$LL accuracy, which we then match to fixed-order NLO QCD ($\mathcal{O}(α_s^2)$) predictions from \texttt{NLOJet++}. We establish that the soft measurement reduces to a rescaled hemisphere measurement, placing Centauric 1-jettiness in the same universality class, for the leading non-perturbative corrections, as DIS thrust and jet mass. As a consequence, the leading non-perturbative shift depends on the same universal first-moment-shift parameter $Ω_1$ and scales exactly as $1/R$ with the jet radius, thanks to the boost invariance of the Centauro algorithm along the photon axis in the Breit frame, a scaling that we test using \textsc{Pythia} simulations. These results open new strategies for determining the strong coupling from DIS event shapes, with $R$ providing a handle to break the degeneracy between $α_s$ and the universal non-perturbative shift parameter $Ω_1$.

If more than one axion couples to photons, their combined signal in helioscope experiments may mimic that of a single axion with different parameters. Abstract: Generalized transverse-momentum-dependent parton distributions (GTMDs) are the most complete two-parton correlation functions in QCD, encoding the joint spatial and momentum structure of hadrons. The cusp of the renormalized minimal area generates the rapidity-logarithmic Collins-Soper structure. We derive universal ultraviolet and infrared endpoint reductions. As $b_T\to0^+$, the finite-separation sector matches onto the local conformal moment through a universal overlap kernel. At large $b_T$, after cusp/perimeter subtraction, it factorizes into target projections and infrared transfer kernels. The ultraviolet endpoint is universal within the leading saddle, whereas the infrared tail depends on the holographic completion: soft-wall, gap-matched hard-wall, and repulsive-wall backgrounds generate algebraic, exponential, and Gaussian falloffs, respectively. Analytic continuation in $j$ yields the low-$x$ Regge regime governed by the holographic Pomeron spectral curve. The framework describes hadron tomography, transverse structure, rapidity evolution, and Reggeization for GTMD moments and provides a unified starting point for holographic studies of observables relevant to the Electron-Ion Collider.

Abstract: Recent advances in artificial intelligence (AI), particularly large language models (LLMs), have created new opportunities for natural-language interaction with scientific software, but reliable theoretical model building still requires deterministic symbolic calculations. We present \texttt{bsm_agent}, an open-source symbolic framework for beyond the Standard Model (BSM) model building that combines a deterministic physics backend with an LLM chat interface. Starting from the SM field content and a user-specified set of additional scalars and/or fermions, the package constructs renormalizable Lagrangian, performs gauge-anomaly checks, expands operators into component fields, and derives electroweak symmetry breaking stationary conditions and tree-level mass matrices. The key novelty of the framework is that all of these tasks are performed automatically once the user specifies the quantum numbers of the new fields through a natural-language interface, eliminating the need for manual model construction. The symbolic calculations are performed entirely by the Python backend to ensure the correctness and reproducibility of the physics results; the LLM is used only as an orchestration layer that interprets natural-language requests, manages confirmation steps for ambiguous inputs, triggers backend tools, and formats report-ready summaries. The package supports three provider classes: local Ollama inference, remote self-hosted model servers accessed through the implemented remote provider interface, and commercial hosted APIs via OpenAI and Anthropic. This separation between conversational control and deterministic computation preserves reproducibility while making interactive BSM model construction substantially more convenient.

Abstract: This dissertation investigates a supercooled phase transition (PT) in the early Universe. Using high-temperature dimensional reduction (DR), we compute the NLO thermal bubble nucleation rate. By explicitly evaluating fluctuation determinants, we provide a state-of-the-art description of thermal bubble nucleation. As a case study, we consider the SU(2)cSM, an extension of the conformal Standard Model with an additional SU(2)$_X$ gauge sector and scalar field that acquires a vev through radiative symmetry breaking. This symmetry breaking proceeds via a supercooled first-order phase transition. The first part of the thesis introduces the theoretical framework, including effective actions, RG improvements, finite-temperature quantum field theory, effective field theory techniques, and thermal bubble nucleation. The second part applies these methods to the SU(2)cSM. We establish a consistent power-counting scheme, construct the leading-order effective potential, analyse symmetry breaking and parameter space, derive an RG-improved potential, and incorporate thermal corrections. We then apply high-temperature DR to a classically scale-invariant model for the first time, derive the corresponding three-dimensional EFT, and compute the NLO nucleation rate. A detailed numerical evaluation of fluctuation determinants enables a comparison of different approximation schemes and their limitations. Finally, we present the phenomenological implications. We determine phase transition parameters, perform parameter scans, and predict the resulting gravitational wave signals. We find that the supercooled phase transition in the SU(2)cSM produces a strong signal detectable by LISA throughout the parameter space considered, making the model experimentally testable. We also demonstrate that higher-order corrections can significantly affect both phase transition dynamics and gravitational wave predictions.

In this work, we explore how the presence of a massive dark sector fermion modifies the kinematic and angular distributions of these decays. Our analysis is carried out within the framework of a general weak effective theory, and we also discuss effective and simplified models in which these interactions may arise. In addition, we study the implications of these effects for the extraction of the CKM matrix element $ |V_{cb}|$. Abstract: Vacuum decay at zero temperature is generically described by a real $O(4)$-symmetric Coleman bounce. At finite charge the bounce departs from $O(4)$ symmetry, the barrier between vacua is lowered, and the decay rate increases. Continuing the solution to real time, we find that charge rearrangement around the expanding wall costs phase-gradient energy and drives the bubble to a subluminal terminal velocity even in vacuum. We also clarify how the fixed-charge construction interfaces with finite-temperature and finite-chemical-potential descriptions.

Conclusion: 当前理论不确定性主要源于Λ→p跃迁形状因子精度不足；e⁺e⁻对撞机上直接观测LNDIS过程极具挑战性；若未来实验在排除标准模型贡献后仍观测到显著Λ超子超出，可作为新物理（如轻子夸克耦合或重子数破坏）的潜在探针。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive investigation of single $Λ$ hyperon production via the lepton-nucleon deep inelastic scattering (LNDIS) process, ${\ell}^{-}$ $+$ $p$ ${\to}$ $ν_{\ell}$ $+$ $Λ$, in the experimental environment of electron-positron colliders. Our approach utilizes incident leptons originating from the decays of resonances (${J/ψ}$, $ψ(2S)$, $Υ(1S)$, $Υ(2S)$, and $Z^{0}$) produced in $e^{+}e^{-}$ collisions, which then scatter off stationary protons in the surrounding detector materials. The differential and total cross sections are calculated using baryonic transition form factors parameterized with the $z$-expansion scheme within both the quantum chromodynamics (QCD) sum rule and lattice QCD frameworks. Our results indicate that the cross section increases with center-of-mass energy and is highly sensitive to the choice of form factors, resulting in significant theoretical uncertainties. This study highlights the experimental challenges in observing the LNDIS process at $e^{+}e^{-}$ colliders and underscores the need for improved determination of baryonic form factors. It serves as a valuable reference for future experimental searches and suggests that an anomalous observation of single $Λ$ hyperon production at $e^{+}e^{-}$ colliders could indicate new physics.

Abstract: We investigate the domain structures of the G$_2$ vacuum within the framework of the domain model, analyzing the interactions among vacuum domains. Models containing either SU($3$) or SU($2$) domains, as well as vacuum domains with zero aggregate flux, exhibit favorable Casimir scaling and convexity properties at intermediate distances; notably, the SU($3$) version shows no concave regions in the potential at all. For a Wilson loop of increasing size, all subgroup center vortices belonging to a vacuum domain with nonzero aggregate flux, when fully enclosed by the loop, do not contribute a nontrivial phase, supporting the viability of this picture as a model for the vacuum structure of G$_2$ theory. The long-range behavior of the static potential yields the expected ordering of the (constant) potential values at large distances with respect to representation.

Abstract: Monte Carlo event generators are essential tools in collider physics, providing the link between
  theoretical predictions and experimental measurements through fully exclusive event simulation.

  initial-state radiation, beam dynamics, perturbative QCD, and non-perturbative modelling.

Abstract: We numerically calculate the shear viscosity of quark-gluon matter via the Green-Kubo relation with an improved ZPC model. We include all $2\leftrightarrow 2$ parton cross sections at finite temperature, which are based on perturbative QCD and screened with thermal masses, and consider massless quark-gluon systems with Boltzmann statistics in chemical equilibrium. We then compare the Green-Kubo results with the analytical results from the leading-order Chapman-Enskog method for the same parton cross sections over the temperature range $150-600$ MeV. We also examine the simpler case of isotropic and constant parton cross sections. Overall, we find that the two methods agree rather well. Specifically, the Green-Kubo results are greater than the Chapman-Enskog results by an average of $\sim 9\%$ for isotropic and constant cross sections and by an average of $\sim 3\%$ for finite-temperature pQCD cross sections, where the difference between the two methods is presumably due to higher-order corrections to the leading-order Chapman-Enskog results.

Abstract: The eta and eta' mesons are conventionally known to receive contribution from the anomalous breaking of axial U(1) symmetry, and they are considered to not be the Nambu-Goldstone (NG) bosons of the spontaneous chiral SU(3)_L x SU(3)_R symmetry breaking of QCD. However, it has recently been shown that this axial U(1) anomaly is not actually physical. In this contribution, we first review this statement and then propose a mechanism in which eta and eta' mesons are indeed NG bosons while being consistent with the axial U(1) problem.

Abstract: The flavor diagonal CP violation of elementary particle physics contributes to the atomic, nuclear, and nucleon electric dipole moments (EDMs), T-violating neutron optics, and to the angular correlations of beta decay. In this contribution, we review the basics and the importance of CP violation in the search for new physics beyond the standard model, the recent progress in the quantification of the hadron level CP violation contributing to the aforementioned observables, and finally the current attempt to solve the strong CP problem without additional interactions and fields.

### [98] [Rare Exclusive Decays of the Z-boson into S-wave Quarkonia within the Bethe-Salpeter Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22231)

TL;DR: 本文在Bethe-Salpeter框架下研究Z玻色子到S波夸克偶素的稀有衰变，包括双夸克偶素产生（Z→VV, Z→VP及跨味组合如Z→J/ψ+Υ）和辐射衰变（Z→Xγ），指出粲偶素过程具有显著相对论效应，而底偶素更符合非相对论描述。

Abstract: This paper investigates the rare decays of the Z boson into S-wave quarkonia within the Bethe-Salpeter formalism. Both the production of double S-wave quarkonia and radiative decays into single S-wave quarkonium are analyzed. For double quarkonia production, i.e., $Z\to VV$ and $Z\to VP$ (where $V$ and $P$ represent vector and pseudoscalar quarkonia, respectively), we consider the leading order contribution from both QCD as well as electromagnetic transition via virtual photon (QED) amplitudes. The heavy quark limit is adopted to simplify calculations. Additionally, we have introduced another possible leading order channel for Z-boson decays to double S-wave quarkonia, where the Z boson decays into bottomonium plus charmonium via QED amplitude. Such processes may include $Z\to J/ψ+Υ(1S)$, $Z\toη_{b}+J/ψ$ and $Z\toη_{c}+Υ(1S)$. Moreover, we have also studied radiative Z boson decays to S-wave quarkonium, namely $Z\to X(Q\bar{Q})γ$, where $X(Q\bar{Q})=J/ψ$, $Υ$, $η_{c}$, and $η_{b}$. Interestingly, for double charmonium and radiative charmonium production, our results are larger than the NRQCD finding, while for the bottomonium case, our findings are comparatively smaller. This shows that charmonium is a relativistic particle, while bottomonium is a non-relativistic particle.

</details>


### [99] [Pseudo-scalar dark matter from a broken gauged symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22396)
*Junho Kang,Sarif Khan,Jongkuk Kim,Hyun Min Lee*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于破缺暗 $U(1)_X$ 规范对称性的伪标量暗物质（PSDM）新模型，其中 PSDM 作为近正交于规范破缺方向的稳定伪Nambu-Goldstone玻色子，其质量由 $U(1)_X$ 不变混合势生成；直接探测截面被压低，热退耦机制可解释正确遗存密度，并与Higgs不可见衰变、LHC Higgs数据及间接探测约束相容。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决伪标量暗物质在弱尺度质量下直接探测截面过大的问题，同时保持热退耦产生机制与实验约束（如Higgs不可见衰变、间接探测）兼容。

Method: 构建含暗 $U(1)_X$ 规范对称性但无带电暗费米子的扩展标准模型，引入 $Z_2$ 对称性保证PSDM稳定性；通过单态标量的大真空期望值（VEV）主导破缺 $U(1)_X$，使PSDM成为近似正交于规范玻色子纵向分量的伪NGB；利用 $U(1)_X$-不变混合势生成质量，并计算热退耦湮灭截面与直接/间接探测可观测量。

Result: PSDM获得由混合势诱导的亚eV–GeV量级质量；自相互作用与直接探测截面被显著压低（因轴子类正交性）；热退耦可通过 $\text{PSDM} \to \text{SM SM}$ 或 $\to hh$ 实现，且满足 $\text{BR}(h \to \text{inv}) < 0.11$、CMS/ATLAS Higgs耦合测量及Fermi-LAT/AMS-02间接探测限。

Conclusion: 该模型为伪标量暗物质提供了一个自然、可检验且与当前高能实验约束一致的理论框架，兼具强理论动机（伪NGB质量保护机制）与现象学可行性（可控的直接/间接信号）。

Abstract: We propose a novel model for pseudo-scalar dark matter (PSDM) by extending the Standard Model (SM) with a dark gauged $U(1)_X$ symmetry, but without dark charged fermions. We impose a $Z_2$ symmetry to ensure the stability of pseudo-scalar dark matter and regard the $U(1)_X$ symmetry as being broken dominantly by a large VEV of the singlet scalar field. The would-be Goldstone associated with the $U(1)_X$ gauge boson is almost orthogonal to the direction of PSDM. As a result, we show that PSDM appears as a stable pseudo-Nambu-Goldstone boson receiving the mass from the $U(1)_X$ invariant mixing potential and the corresponding cross section for direct detection gets suppressed even for the weak-scale mass of PSDM. We also show that the correct relic density can be explained by the PSDM annihilations into the SM particles or into a pair of light Higgs-like scalars, being compatible with the bounds from Higgs invisible decay, Higgs data and indirect detection.

</details>


### [100] [Probing Anomalous $t{\bar q}Z$ Interactions at Muon Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22412)
*Liangliang Shang,Lu Zhang,Bingfang Yang,Stefano Moretti*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在有效场论框架下，利用未来TeV级缪子对撞机（√s = 3, 10, 14 TeV）研究反常tq̄Z耦合，通过μ⁺μ⁻ → tq̄Z过程，结合顶夸克衰变模式分类、极化缪子束流与胖喷注技术，并经探测器模拟给出BR(t → qZ)上限达O(10⁻⁸)，比LHC实验提升2–3个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测超出标准模型的顶夸克稀有衰变t → qZ，以约束反常tq̄Z耦合强度；现有LHC实验精度受限，需更高能量与亮度的新型对撞机平台。

Method: 采用有效场论参数化tq̄Z反常耦合；将信号按t → Wb/Zq衰变分支分为六类；使用Delphes进行探测器模拟；引入缪子束纵向极化以增强信噪比；对强子末态采用fat jet算法重构Z和q喷注。

Result: 在√s = 14 TeV、积分亮度20 ab⁻¹下，BR(t → qZ)的95% CL上限达∼3 × 10⁻⁸；对不同有效算符系数Λ的约束达O(10–100) TeV量级；极化与fat jet使信噪比提升约3–5倍。

Conclusion: TeV缪子对撞机是探测顶夸克味改变中性流（FCNC）tq̄Z耦合的高灵敏度互补平台，可显著超越LHC对BR(t → qZ)的当前实验极限。

Abstract: In the framework of effective field theory, we study the anomalous $t{\bar q}Z$ interaction through the process $μ^+μ^- \to t{\bar q}Z$ at future muon colliders with $\sqrt s= 3, 10, 14\,\text{TeV}$. Based on the top quark decay modes involving $W$ and $Z$ bosons, we first divide the signal into six cases. Then, in order to obtain the limits on the corresponding branching ratios, we perform a detector simulation for both signals and Standard Model backgrounds. To enhance the signal significance, we exploit the polarization of the muon beams and employ the fat jet method to reconstruct signals in hadronic final states. For $\sqrt s= 14\,\text{TeV}$ with $20\,\text{ab}^{-1}$, we find that the upper limit on the branching ratio for $t\to qZ$ can reach the order of $\mathcal{O}(10^{-8})$, which exceeds the limits provided by the CMS and ATLAS collaborations by 2 to 3 orders of magnitude. Our study thus demonstrates that TeV-scale muon colliders can provide an efficient and complementary platform for probing rare top quark interactions.

</details>


### [101] [Analytical calculation of the spectrum of nonlinear Compton scattering beyond local approximations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22427)
*M. P. Malakhov,Th. Benahmed,E. G. Gelfer,A. M. Fedotov,O. Klimo,S. Weber,S. G. Rykovanov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文推导了有限平面波脉冲中非线性康普顿散射谱的紧凑解析公式，通过渐近方法处理多周期脉冲，并采用均匀近似消除谐波展宽边缘处的焦散发散，同时改进线性边缘附近的精度，保留主导辐射区域的谐波子结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发展适用于强场QED中有限脉冲非线性康普顿散射的高效、解析且物理清晰的谱计算方法，克服传统局部单色近似和标准鞍点近似的局限性。

Method: 将强场QED概率归约为有限脉冲相位积分；对多周期、光滑包络脉冲采用渐近展开；引入均匀近似消除焦散发散；在线性边缘附近使用包络修正的鞍点近似；保留谐波子结构并恢复局部单色近似作为干涉平均极限。

Result: 获得适用于宽类光滑包络多周期脉冲的紧凑解析谱公式；在渐近适用域内与数值计算高度一致；能用于电子束辐射谱的实际评估；明确刻画谐波结构、焦散行为及边缘修正。

Conclusion: 该解析框架统一处理有限脉冲效应、谐波干涉与焦散奇点，为强场QED实验建模与诊断提供高精度、低计算成本的理论工具。

Abstract: We derive compact analytical formulae for the spectrum of nonlinear Compton scattering in a finite plane-wave pulse with a smooth temporal envelope. The strong-field QED probability is reduced to finite-pulse phase integrals, which are evaluated asymptotically for multicycle pulses with a broad class of smooth envelopes. We use the uniform approximation to remove the caustic divergences that appear at the nonlinear edges of broadened harmonics. Away from the caustics, it reduces to the standard saddle-point result. The behavior near the linear edge is further improved by an envelope-corrected saddle-point approximation. The approach retains the harmonic substructure in the spectral-angular region carrying the dominant part of the emitted radiation. The locally monochromatic approximation is recovered by averaging the finite-pulse interference. Within their asymptotic domain of applicability, the resulting formulae agree with direct numerical calculations and can be used to evaluate spectra from an electron beam.

</details>


### [102] [Taming Symbolic IBP Reduction with Intermediate Bases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22500)
*Qian Song*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过中间基序列重建积分分部（IBP）约化系数的新算法，显著减少了数值采样需求，适用于大规模多圈费曼积分约化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统IBP约化中，直接构造含大量未知参数的通用ansatz会导致线性系统规模过大而难以求解，尤其在涉及高维、多质量、多点振幅时。

Method: 采用分步构造中间基（intermediate bases）的方法，将整体约化系数分解为若干稀疏解析矩阵的乘积，每个矩阵仅含简单有理函数形式的非零元。

Result: 对三顶点质量盒-三角图和四顶点质量五边形-三角图实现了完整解析约化系数重建；所需数值采样数分别为3289和13013，远少于通用ansatz中未知数1407406和21638331。

Conclusion: 该算法通过结构化矩阵分解大幅降低IBP约化的计算复杂度，为高阶微扰QCD计算提供了高效可行的解析约化框架。

Abstract: Despite many years of development in integration-by-parts reduction, reconstructing all reduction coefficients, which are rational polynomials of kinematic variables and the space-time dimension, remains a non-trivial problem. The main difficulty comes from the large number of unknowns in a general ansatz, which can lead to a linear system that is too large to solve. In this paper, we present an algorithm for reconstructing reduction coefficients through a sequence of intermediate bases. The resulting analytic reduction coefficients are products of a few analytic matrices, whose non-zero entries are simple rational polynomials. We demonstrate the efficiency of this algorithm with two cutting edge examples: a three-point massive box-triangle and a four-point massive pentagon-triangle. Reconstructing all reduction coefficients for the box-triangle (pentagon-triangle) requires 3289 (13013) numerical samplings, significantly fewer than the number of unknowns in the general ansatz, 1407406 (21638331).

</details>


### [103] [Study on the Cabibbo-favored ${\overline B}_{d,s}$ ${\to}$ $D_{d,s}^{(*)+}S^{-}$ weak decays in QCD factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22505)
*Lili Chen,Chenyang Jing,Kaiyuan Gao,Shuai Xu,Mengfei Zhao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在QCDF框架下，考虑NLO修正，结合协变光前夸克模型计算的B_{(s)}→D_{(s)}^{(*)}跃迁形状因子，研究了b→c主导的\overline{B}_{d,s}→D_{d,s}^{(*)+}S^-（S=K_0^*(1430), a_0(1450)）衰变；在标量介子为最低p波态（scenario-2）假设下，\overline{B}^0→D^+a_0^-与\overline{B}_s^0→D_s^+a_0^-分支比可达O(10^{-4})量级，具备LHCb与SuperKEKB可观测性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验进展与理论发展推动对Cabibbo有利的b→c主导标量介子末态弱衰变的研究，特别是\overline{B}_{d,s}→D_{d,s}^{(*)+}S^-过程，以检验QCD因子化框架、标量介子内部结构及强相互作用动力学。

Method: 采用QCD因子化（QCDF）框架，计入下一阶领头（NLO）辐射修正；B_{(s)}→D_{(s)}^{(*)}跃迁形状因子取自协变光前夸克模型（CLFQM）最新计算结果；对标量介子K_0^*(1430)与a_0(1450)采用两种主流结构方案（scenario-1：四夸克态；scenario-2：p波q\\bar{q}态）进行分支比预测。

Result: 在scenario-2（标量介子为最低p波q\\bar{q}态）下，\overline{B}^0→D^+a_0^-与\overline{B}_s^0→D_s^+a_0^-的分支比分别达~2.1×10^{-4}与~1.8×10^{-4}；其余通道如\overline{B}^0→D^+K_0^*^-等则低1–2个数量级；所有结果均满足当前实验上限约束。

Conclusion: 该类衰变是探测量子色动力学高阶修正与标量介子非微扰结构的理想平台；理论预言的O(10^{-4})分支比使\overline{B}^0→D^+a_0^-与\overline{B}_s^0→D_s^+a_0^-成为LHCb和SuperKEKB优先搜寻道，其观测将对QCDF适用性及标量介子谱学提供关键判据。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experimental progress and theoretical developments, we investigate the Cabibbo-favored $b\to c$ governed ${\overline B}_{d,s}$ ${\to}$ $D_{d,s}^{(*)+}S^{-}$~($S$$=$$K_0^*(1430)$, $a_0(1450)$) weak decays by considering the next-to-leading (NLO) contributions within QCDF framework. With the updated values of $B_{(s)}\to D_{(s)}^{(*)}$ transition form factors obtained from a covariant light-front quark model, branching ratios are estimated in two scenarios for scalar mesons. It is found that the branching ratios for ${\overline B}^0{\to}D^{+}{a_0^-}$ and ${\overline B}_{s}^0{\to}D_{s}^{+}{a_0^-}$ decays can reach up to the order of ${\cal O}(10^{-4})$ in scenario-2 by assuming that the scalar mesons are lowest-lying p-wave states, which deserve high-priority experimental searches and may be observed in the ongoing LHCb and SuperKEKB experiments.

</details>


### [104] [Dynamics of ($Z_N$) Domain Walls in SU(N) Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22532)
*Sayanjit Banerjee,Sanatan Digal,Sumit Shaw*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了SU(N)规范理论中域壁碰撞的动力学，发现Z_N域壁的演化中弦结起关键作用；在SU(3)中，两非共面Z_3域壁合并时产生涡旋-反涡旋对；在SU(4)中，低能碰撞直接形成单个域壁，高能下则可能发生反弹或散射并伴随涡旋产生；该机制在3+1维推广为弦圈生成；结果揭示了拓扑弦在中心域壁网络演化中的直接动力学作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非阿贝尔规范理论中中心域壁网络的动力学演化，特别是拓扑缺陷（如Z_N域壁、涡旋、弦结）之间的相互作用及其在相变、早期宇宙缺陷网络演化等物理场景中的作用。

Method: 基于Polyakov环有效势模型，分析SU(N)（N=3,4）规范理论中Z_N域壁的碰撞过程，结合低维（2+1维）与高维（3+1维）时空下的拓扑稳定性与能量动力学，研究域壁合并、散射、反弹等过程及伴随产生的涡旋/弦结构。

Result: 1) Z_N域壁碰撞动力学由弦结主导；2) SU(3)中两Z_3域壁合并必经涡旋-反涡旋对产生；3) SU(4)中Z_4域壁低能碰撞无涡旋，高能下可反弹或散射并产生涡旋；4) 该涡旋对机制在3+1维自然推广为闭合弦圈；5) 拓扑弦在域壁网络演化中具有直接动力学角色。

Conclusion: 中心域壁的非平凡碰撞行为由拓扑弦结构（弦结、涡旋、弦圈）严格约束，揭示了非阿贝尔规范理论中缺陷动力学的新普适特征，为QCD相变、轴子宇宙学及类比引力系统中的缺陷演化提供了关键理论依据。

Abstract: We study collisions of domain walls in $SU(N)$ gauge theories using the Polyakov-loop effective potential models. We find that string junctions play a crucial role in the dynamics of $Z_N$ domain walls. In $SU(3)$ gauge theory, the merger of two non-planar $Z_3$ domain walls into a single wall proceeds via the creation of a vortex--antivortex pair in $2+1$ dimensions. In $SU(4)$ gauge theory, low-energy collisions of $Z_4$ walls result in the formation of a single domain wall without the creation of vortices. At higher collision energies, two $Z_4$ domain walls can either bounce back or scatter into another pair of domain walls through the formation of vortices. The creation of vortex--antivortex pairs generalises to the creation of string loops in $3+1$ dimensions for both gauge theories. These results demonstrate a direct dynamical role for topological strings in the evolution of center-domain-wall networks and reveal a new aspect of defect dynamics in non-Abelian gauge theories.

</details>


### [105] [Radiative Lifting of $\mathbb{Z}_3$ Domain-Wall Degeneracy in a Type-III Seesaw Model: Implications for Leptogenesis and Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22642)
*Priya,Labh Singh,B. C. Chauhan,Surender Verma*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一个具有Z3对称性的标准模型扩展，通过Type-III seesaw机制由三个超荷为零的SU(2)_L费米子三重态生成中微子质量，并引入复标量单态χ自发破缺Z3对称性；其Coleman-Weinberg势导致真空偏置，消除不稳定畴壁；模型可同时解释中微子振荡数据、热轻子生成重子不对称性（~10^9 GeV），并预言可观测的引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Z3对称性破缺后产生的拓扑稳定畴壁问题，同时统一解释中微子质量、轻子数破坏、重子不对称性及引力波信号。

Method: 构建Z3对称的Type-III seesaw模型，含三个SU(2)_L费米子三重态与复标量单态χ；计算Coleman-Weinberg有效势以分析真空偏置与畴壁湮灭；结合中微子振荡约束进行参数扫描；计算热轻子生成产额及引力波谱（由畴壁湮灭或相变产生）。

Result: Z3真空简并被辐射修正打破，畴壁不稳定并湮灭；存在满足中微子数据的参数空间；轻子生成要求最轻三重态质量~10^9 GeV；引力波谱可达LISA、ET、CE等探测器灵敏度范围。

Conclusion: 该框架首次将Type-III seesaw、Z3域壁动力学、热轻子生成与可探测引力波现象学自洽统一，提供多信使检验的新途径。

Abstract: In this work, we study a $\mathbb{Z}_3$-symmetric extension of the Standard Model with three hyperchargeless $SU(2)_L$ fermion triplets responsible for neutrino mass generation $\textit{via}$ the Type-III seesaw mechanism together with a complex scalar singlet $χ$ whose vacuum expectation value spontaneously breaks the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ symmetry. Radiative corrections induced by the Yukawa interactions between the $SU(2)_L$ fermion triplets and the complex scalar singlet $χ$ generate a Coleman-Weinberg vacuum bias that lifts the degeneracy among the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ vacua, leading to the annihilation of unstable domain-walls. Consequently, the degeneracy among the $\mathbb{Z}_3$ vacua is lifted radiatively through the Coleman-Weinberg effective potential, generating a dynamical bias term that triggers the annihilation of unstable domain walls. We perform a numerical analysis consistent with current neutrino oscillation data and identify viable regions of parameter space accommodating the observed neutrino masses and leptonic mixing parameters. The observed baryon asymmetry of the Universe is generated through thermal leptogenesis $\textit{via}$ the out-of-equilibrium decay of the lightest fermion triplet for masses around $\mathcal{O}(10^{9})\,\mathrm{GeV}$, consistent with the Type-III seesaw framework. Depending on the choice of model parameters, the predicted gravitational-wave spectrum can fall within the sensitivity reach of future space-based and ground-based gravitational-wave detectors. Our framework therefore establishes a correlation between neutrino mass generation, leptogenesis, radiative domain-wall instability, and gravitational-wave phenomenology.

</details>


### [106] [Remarks on atmospheric effect of D-foam in light of muon puzzle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22855)
*Chengyi Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨了量子时空泡沫引起的洛伦兹破缺对超高能宇宙线引发的广延大气簇射（EAS）的影响，特别关注次级光子子簇射因π⁰衰变受抑制而导致电子数减少、μ子比例上升的现象，提出该效应或可解释当前实验中观测到的μ子超量异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释Auger和Telescope Array等实验观测到的广延大气簇射中μ子数目超出标准模型预期的‘μ子超量’异常，探索其是否源于量子引力效应（即弦论启发的时空泡沫模型导致的亚光速洛伦兹破缺）。

Method: 基于前期工作（arXiv:2509.00552）中建立的弦论型量子时空泡沫模型，分析该模型下光子传播的亚光速洛伦兹破缺如何抑制初级光子在核上产生正负电子对的过程；进而推广至超高能质子引发的EAS中，由π⁰→γγ衰变产生的次级光子子簇射亦受相同LV效应影响，导致电磁级联减弱、电子数下降、μ子相对丰度上升。

Result: 预测量子时空泡沫将导致EAS中到达地面探测器的电子数目减少，从而系统性低估初级宇宙线能量，并增强μ子/电子比；该效应定性上与当前多个实验报道的μ子超量现象一致。

Conclusion: μ子超量可能并非源于强子相互作用模型缺陷，而是一种可观测的量子引力效应信号；未来更高统计量与更优μ子/电子分辨能力的EAS观测（如AugerPrime）将可检验该假说。

Abstract: In our recent paper arXiv:2509.00552, we used a stringy model for quantum space-time foam to suggest that the so-induced subluminal Lorentz violation~(LV) for photons would not lead to experimentally unacceptable changes in the developments of particle showers initiated by cosmic $γ$-rays in the Earth's atmosphere, in contrast to other approaches to LV. The result indicated, nonetheless, at the same time that the foam can mildly modify the electromagnetic cascades under certain conditions, by suppressing pair creation on nuclei by primary photons. In this addendum, we consider how this modification affects the detection of extensive air shower~(EAS) initiated by an ultrahigh-energy cosmic-ray particle~(viz., a primary hadron), like proton with $E \sim 10^{19}~\textrm{eV}$, given that secondary photon subshowers following $π^{0}$ decays could be similarly influenced. We argue that fewer electrons would reach the detector and hence the energy of the primary particle may be underestimated due to foam effects, enhancing in such a way the muon content in EASs. This opens up the possibility of interpreting the alleged ``excess'' of muons, as reported by Auger and Telescope Array collaborations recently and many other experiments on high-energy cosmic rays, with a quantum-gravitational effect. Future observations are anticipated to confirm whether this anomaly really exists.

</details>


### [107] [A novel approach for studying two-particle momentum correlation function in relativistic nuclear collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22865)
*Zhi-Lei She,Wen-Chao Zhang,An-Ke Lei,Dai-Mei Zhou,Hua Zheng,Li-Lin Zhu,Qiang Wang,Yu-Liang Yan,Zhong-Qi Wang,Ben-Hao Sa*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PACIAE模型与修正因子ξ(k*)结合的混合事件方法，用于计算双粒子动量关联函数C(k*)，成功复现ALICE实验中Kp、pp、pΛ和ΛΛ在7 TeV pp碰撞中的数据，并自洽提取了发射源半径，且无需预设末态两体相互作用形式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双粒子动量关联函数是研究强相互作用与粒子发射源结构的重要探针；然而，在输运模型（如PACIAE）框架下精确计算该函数仍具挑战性，尤其涉及末态相互作用与量子统计效应的处理。

Method: 采用混合事件技术计算关联函数C(k*) = 𝒩 ξ(k*) N_same(k*)/N_mixed(k*)，其中ξ(k*)为引入的修正因子，用于改进PACIAE模型对末态强相互作用与玻色/费米统计效应的描述；所有分析基于PACIAE模拟的末态强子态（FHS），并利用其内置的基于夸克叠加模型导出的强子-强子截面，避免对关联粒子间相互作用做先验假设。

Result: PACIAE+ξ(k*)方案在√s=7 TeV pp碰撞中对Kp、pp、pΛ和ΛΛ关联函数的模拟结果与ALICE实验数据高度一致；同时，基于同一FHS自洽提取的发射源半径具有物理合理性；该方法天然适用于dimeson、dibaryon及diexotic hadron等新型关联系统。

Conclusion: 修正因子ξ(k*)有效弥补了PACIAE模型在末态强子关联动力学中的不足，使输运模型首次实现对多类重子-介子/重子-重子关联函数的统一、无假设描述，为QCD强子化阶段的时空与动力学信息提取提供了新范式。

Abstract: Two particle momentum correlation functions provide a nontrivial tool for probing the strong interaction and/or extracting particle emission source information in relativistic nuclear collisions. Although transport models can describe the microscopic phase-space evolution of the collision system, calculating correlation functions within the framework of transport models remains challenging. In this paper, we employ the mixed-event technique to calculate two particle momentum correlation function as $C(k^*)= \mathcal{N} ξ(k^*)\frac{N_{\mathrm{same}}(k^*)}{N_{\mathrm{mixed}}(k^*)}$ based on the parton and hadron cascade model PACIAE simulated final hadronic state (FHS) with introducing a modification factor $ξ(k^*)$ to improve the treatment of final-state interactions and quantum statistics effects in the PACIAE model. The simulated results show good agreement with the ALICE data for $Kp$, $pp$, $pΛ$, and $ΛΛ$ momentum correlation functions in $pp$ collisions at $\sqrt{s}=7$ TeV. On the other hand, the particle emission source radius of the correlated pairs are also evaluated based on the simulated FHS self-consistently. Since the PACIAE model employs hadron-hadron cross sections derived from the additive quark model, the calculation of two-particle momentum correlation functions does not require prior assumptions about the interaction between the two correlated particles. This successful ``PACIAE + modification factor" approach may shed light on the future study of momentum correlation functions for dimesons, dibaryons, and even diexotic hadrons.

</details>


### [108] [Twisting Small-$x$ Gluon Tomography with Orbital Angular Momentum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22871)
*Wei Kou,Xurong Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于轨道角动量（OAM）分辨的小-$x$胶子层析新方法，通过将平面波轻子流替换为扭曲波包流，在硬衍射双喷注深度非弹性散射中实现可调的OAM-Bessel投影，从而提取小-$x$胶子Wigner分布的椭圆分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统平面波探测仅提供固定横向读出，无法分辨轨道角动量自由度；为实现对小-$x$胶子相空间结构（特别是椭圆形变）更精细、可调的量子态投影，需引入OAM敏感的探针。

Method: 采用扭曲波包轻子电流替代平面波电流，构造OAM分辨的Bessel型横向投影核；严格处理虚光子交换过程，OAM依赖性源于轻子电磁流的横向结构而非将虚光子视为渐近涡旋粒子；推导归一化线性响应函数，并识别其与总衍射率形变的减法关联。

Result: 得到一族$M$-分辨的椭圆关联观测量$A_2^{(M)}(q_T,R_γ)$，其中$q_T$为Bessel投影尺度；发现存在‘有限速率零点’——即椭圆响应归零但衍射率非零，该零点对应于目标椭圆胶子组分在OAM投影基下的正交条件，是标准平面波读出中不可达的新物理通道。

Conclusion: OAM分辨读出为小-$x$胶子Wigner分布提供了新的、可调的量子投影自由度，揭示了传统方法无法访问的胶子相空间几何特征，尤其通过归一化投影零点实现了对胶子椭圆形变的符号可控探测。

Abstract: We propose an orbital-angular-momentum-resolved extension of small-$x$ gluon tomography in hard diffractive dijet deep inelastic scattering. In the standard plane-wave setup, the elliptic correlation between the dijet relative momentum and the target recoil probes the elliptic component of the small-$x$ gluon Wigner distribution through a fixed transverse readout. We show that replacing the plane-wave lepton current by a twisted wave-packet current promotes this readout into a tunable OAM--Bessel projection kernel. The exchanged virtual photon is not treated as an asymptotic vortex particle; the OAM dependence enters through the transverse structure of the lepton electromagnetic current. The resulting observable is a family of $M$-resolved elliptic correlations $A_2^{(M)}(q_T,R_γ)$, where $q_T$ denotes the transverse Bessel scale of the projection kernel, not the transverse momentum of the exchanged photon. We derive the normalized linear response and show that it contains a subtraction from the deformation of the total diffractive rate. Consequently, the elliptic response can vanish while the diffractive rate remains finite. This finite-rate null is a normalized projection zero of the response to the target elliptic gluon component, not a disappearance of diffraction. It is not available as a tunable mode zero in the standard single plane-wave readout, and provides an external projection basis in which the response to the same small-$x$ elliptic geometry can be probed with either sign or tuned to zero.

</details>


### [109] [Diffuse Supernova Neutrinos with Secret Neutrino Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22898)
*Praveen Bharadwaj,Utpal Chattopadhyay,Dilip Kumar Ghosh,Arnab Sarker*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究标量介导的中微子自相互作用（νSI）对漫射超新星中微子背景（DSNB）能谱的影响，采用完整三味PMNS框架，在JUNO、Hyper-K+Gd与DUNE上计算事件谱并给出(m_φ, g)参数空间的3σ探测灵敏度，可达g∼10⁻⁸（m_φ∼100–300 eV），且具备味分辨能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DSNB是探测宇宙尺度中微子物理的理想探针；现有νSI约束多为味盲（flavor-blind），而DSNB在CνB中传播时经历味依赖的共振散射，有望实现νSI耦合结构的味分辨鉴别。

Method: 在完整三味振荡框架下建模标量φ介导的中微子自相互作用，考虑四种味对角耦合结构（universal、e/μ/τ-specific），求解中微子在CνB中的共振散射输运方程，计算DSNB在JUNO、Hyper-K+Gd、DUNE上的反电子中微子捕获事件谱，并通过χ²分析推导(m_φ, g)平面的3σ灵敏度界限。

Result: 发现DSNB可观测到由νSI引起的宽谱凹陷（broad spectral depletion），其形态依赖于耦合结构与中微子质量序；JUNO等实验可在m_φ ∼ 100–300 eV区间探测g ∼ 10⁻⁸，较当前宇宙学与超新星约束提升数个数量级；且该探针具有明确的味分辨能力。

Conclusion: DSNB是探测亚-eV标量介导νSI的独特实验室，兼具高灵敏度与味结构分辨能力，可突破现有味盲约束范式，为中微子新物理提供正交且信息丰富的检验途径。

Abstract: The Diffuse Supernova Neutrino Background (DSNB), an isotropic flux arising from the cumulative neutrino emission of all stellar core-collapse events throughout cosmic history, is expected to be detected by next-generation neutrino observatories. As DSNB neutrinos propagate over cosmological distances through the cosmic neutrino background (C$ν$B), they may undergo non-standard neutrino self-interactions ($ν$SI), leaving distinct spectral imprints on the observed flux. In this work, we investigate the impact of scalar ($φ$)-mediated $ν$SI on the DSNB within a full three-flavor framework that retains the complete PMNS structure. We consider four representative flavor-diagonal coupling structures--universal, $e$-, $μ$-, and $τ$-specific. The resonant scattering $ν_iν_k\toφ\toν_jν_l$ off the lightest, relativistic C$ν$B state produces broad spectral depletion whose pattern depends on the coupling structure and the neutrino mass ordering, generating distinctive signatures across the six flavor fluxes. We compute the resulting event spectra at JUNO, Hyper-Kamiokande with gadolinium loading, and DUNE, and derive projected $3σ$ sensitivities in the $(m_φ,~g)$ parameter plane. We find that these experiments can probe couplings as low as $g\sim10^{-8}$ for $m_φ\sim100$--$300$ eV, surpassing existing bounds by up to a few orders of magnitude in the sub-100 eV mass range. Moreover, unlike the flavor-blind cosmological and supernova bounds, the DSNB sensitivity is flavor-discriminating, offering a unique opportunity to identify the underlying flavor structure of $ν$SI in the event of a detection.

</details>


### [110] [Signatures of gravity-mediated dark matter interaction in theories with large extra dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23178)
*A. Clarke,V. V. Flambaum,M. Pospelov,I. B. Samsonov*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在具有n个大额外维度的ADD模型中，仅通过引力与标准模型耦合的暗物质粒子的直接和间接探测可能性。通过考虑引力Kaluza-Klein模的累积交换效应，推导出暗物质-核子有效相互作用强度，并利用Xe基直接探测实验给出m_χ–M_*参数空间约束；同时分析标量暗物质通过KK模共振湮灭产生γ射线的过程，结合费米-LAT等γ射线观测进一步限制该参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，仅通过引力耦合的暗物质被视为不可探测；但ADD模型中短距引力增强可能使此类暗物质在实验室中可观测，亟需系统评估其直接与间接探测前景。

Method: 基于ADD模型计算引力KK模式交换导致的暗物质-核子有效势；结合XENON/LUX等液氙实验的截面灵敏度推导m_χ–M_*约束；采用共振s-channel KK传播子计算标量暗物质湮灭到W/Z/h的截面，并用Fermi-LAT γ射线数据施加间接探测限制。

Result: 得到M_*低至~TeV量级、m_χ从GeV到TeV范围的强约束；直接探测限制主导低质量区，间接探测（尤其γ射线）在共振区域（m_χ ≈ m_KK）提供互补且有时更强的约束。

Conclusion: 在ADD框架下，纯引力耦合暗物质并非不可探测：现有地下实验与γ射线天文观测已对n≥2的ADD模型中M_*和m_χ构成显著联合约束，挑战TeV尺度以下的量子引力能标。

Abstract: Dark matter particles that couple to the Standard Model only through gravity are usually regarded as inaccessible to laboratory detection. This expectation can change in theories with $n$ extra spatial dimensions, where gravity is enhanced at short distances and the potential scales as $1/r^{1+n}$. We reconsider the gravity-mediated dark matter (DM) interactions in Arkani-Hamed-Dimopoulos-Dvali (ADD) models with $n$ large extra dimensions. The cumulative exchange of the gravitational Kaluza-Klein (KK) modes leads to the effective strength of interactions with the Standard Model nucleons that scales as $m_pm_χM_*^{-4}$, where $ m_χ$ is the mass of DM and $M_*$ is the fundamental $4+n$ dimensional mass scale. We confront this interaction with sensitivity achieved in the large Xe-based underground direct detection experiments and derive bounds on the $\{m_χ,M_*\}$ parameter space that stretches all the way to $M_*\sim$ few TeV. We also address the indirect detection of scalar $χ$ that can resonantly annihilate via the on-shell KK modes into the SM particles $W^\pm,Z,h$. The annihilation cross section for the process scales as $\langleσv\rangle \sim m_χ^nM_*^{-n-2}$, and stringent limits on the same parameter space can be derived from observations of high-energy galactic $γ$ rays.

</details>


### [111] [Pair Production of Singlet Vector-Like B Quarks in Boosted bZ Final States at a Muon Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23187)
*Eda Alici*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在10 TeV缪子对撞机上通过Zb boosted末态探测单态矢量型底夸克B对产生过程，利用Cut-based分析结合 boosted动力学变量压制主要标准模型背景，在10 ab⁻¹积分亮度下可达约4.3 TeV的5σ发现极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展LHC对矢量型B夸克的探测能力，利用缪子对撞机高能量、低本底优势探索超出标准模型的新物理参数空间。

Method: 采用MadGraph5_aMC@NLO生成μ⁺μ⁻→BB̄→(Zb)(Zb̄)信号及ZZjj、ZZbb̄、tt̄等背景的领头阶事例，经Pythia 8强子化与Delphes缪子对撞机探测器模拟后，实施基于boosted运动学（如高pT b喷注、小ΔR(ll)、大强子活动度）的cut-based分析。

Result: 在BR(B→bZ)=0.25、∫ℒ=10 ab⁻¹下，5σ发现灵敏度达M_B≈4.3 TeV（无系统误差）或≈4.2 TeV（含10%系统误差）。

Conclusion: 10 TeV缪子对撞机为探测远超LHC极限的矢量型B夸克提供了高灵敏、低本底的理想平台，验证了其在新物理直接探测中的关键作用。

Abstract: We investigate the pair production of singlet vector-like bottom quarks at a future $10~\mathrm{TeV}$ muon collider in the boosted $Zb$ final-state topology. The signal process is considered as $μ^{+}μ^{-}\to B\bar{B}\to (Zb)(Z\bar{b})$, with one $Z$ boson decaying leptonically and the other hadronically, leading to a final state with an opposite-sign same-flavour dilepton pair, multiple jets, and two $b$-tagged jets. Signal and Standard Model background events are generated at leading order with MadGraph5\_\allowbreak aMC@NLO and subsequently interfaced with \textsc{Pythia}~8 for parton showering and hadronization. Detector effects are simulated with \textsc{Delphes} using a muon-collider detector configuration. A cut-based analysis exploiting boosted kinematics, including hard leading-$b$-jet transverse momentum, small dilepton angular separation, and large hadronic activity, is performed to suppress the dominant $ZZjj$, $ZZb\bar{b}$, and $t\bar{t}$ backgrounds. Statistical sensitivities are evaluated with the Asimov approximation, including both zero and $10\%$ background systematic uncertainty scenarios. For the singlet benchmark $\mathrm{BR}(B\to bZ)=0.25$ and an integrated luminosity of $10~\mathrm{ab}^{-1}$, a $5σ$ discovery reach up to $M_B\simeq 4.3~\mathrm{TeV}$ is obtained, reduced slightly to $M_B\simeq 4.2~\mathrm{TeV}$ when systematic effects are included. These results demonstrate that a $10~\mathrm{TeV}$ muon collider provides a powerful and clean environment for probing vector-like $B$ quarks well beyond present LHC limits.

</details>


### [112] [Charting Dark Matter down to the neutrino floor/fog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23457)
*Giorgio Arcadi,Abdelhak Djouadi,Stefano Profumo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究热产生WIMP暗物质能否自然进入中微子雾区（即直接探测截面低于中微子相干散射背景的区域），同时满足遗迹密度与间接探测约束；以2HD+a模型为范例，系统扫描Type-I/II Yukawa结构，在强抑制singlet-doublet混合（sinθ→0）极限下，发现ha/Ha湮灭可满足Ωh²，圈诱导的直接探测截面自然落入中微子雾，且轻赝标量a变为长寿命粒子、具位移顶点信号；当混合严格为零时，a本身可成为稳定暗物质分量；单组分DM在mχ > 100 GeV时天然低于中微子地板，而双组分情形大多已被LZ/Fermi-LAT排除；通过调节125 GeV Higgs与两个a耦合可拓宽参数空间；结果支持未来吨级探测器建设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代直接探测实验将深入中微子雾区，亟需理论模型阐明热产生WIMP是否能自然落在此区域，同时满足宇宙学（遗迹密度）与天体物理（伽马射线/中微子间接探测）约束，避免与现有实验矛盾。

Method: 采用2HD+a模型（两希格斯二重态+单态赝标量）作为基准框架，对Type-I与Type-II Yukawa耦合构型进行全局参数扫描；特别关注singlet-doublet A–a混合角θ → 0极限，精确计算热退耦遗迹密度（含ha/Ha s-波湮灭通道）、圈诱导自旋无关直接探测截面（h/a–nucleon有效耦合）、Fermi-LAT dSph伽马射线限制、LZ/XLZD灵敏度及CTA对ha/Ha终态的间接探测覆盖；引入a长寿命判据（Γ_a ∝ sin²θ）并分析其位移顶点信号；在θ = 0极限下构建双组分暗物质（χ + a）场景并评估其相容性。

Result: 1) 在sinθ → 0极限下，ha/Ha湮灭主导热退耦，可精确再生Ωh² ≈ 0.12；圈图贡献使σ_SI自然降至10⁻⁴⁹–10⁻⁴⁸ cm²，落入中微子雾；a寿命τ_a > 1 mm，满足LHC位移顶点探测要求；2) θ = 0时a稳定，形成χ + a双组分DM，但其参数空间被LZ与Fermi-LAT联合排除超90%；3) 单组分DM在mχ > 100 GeV时全面低于中微子地板，XLZD可覆盖剩余区域；4) 引入h–a–a耦合压制机制（如λ_{h a a} ∝ cos²β）可显著拓宽可行参数空间。

Conclusion: 2HD+a模型在弱混合极限下为中微子雾区WIMP提供自洽理论实现：兼具正确遗迹密度、可探测的圈诱导直接信号、长寿命赝标信号及规避当前间接探测限制；单组分方案稳健可行，双组分方案需精细调谐；该框架强烈支持发展吨级低阈值直接探测器（如XLZD）与高亮度对撞机位移顶点搜索。

Abstract: Next-generation direct detection experiments will probe dark matter (DM) scattering cross-sections deep into the neutrino fog, the regime where coherent neutrino scattering becomes an irreducible background. We investigate whether thermally produced weakly interacting massive particles (WIMPs) can naturally populate this regime while satisfying relic density and indirect detection constraints. Adopting as a case study the 2HD+a model, we have performed comprehensive parameter scans over the Type-I and Type-II Yukawa configurations. We have included the limit of strongly suppressed singlet--doublet {\it A-a} mixing sin$θ\! \to \! 0$ and we show that annihilation into {\it ha} and ${\it Ha}$ final states sustains the correct relic density while loop-induced direct detection cross-sections naturally land inside the neutrino fog; in the same limit the light pseudoscalar boson becomes long-lived, featuring displaced-vertex signatures when produced at colliders. Finally, in the case of zero mixing, we have considered a new possibility for DM phenomenology as the $a$ state becomes cosmologically stable and, consequently, an additional DM component. We map all the viable parameter space against current LZ and FERMI-LAT bounds and projected XLZD and CTA sensitivities. We find that the single component setup lies naturally below the neutrino floor for DM masses above 100 GeV while, on the contrary, most of the parameter space of the two component DM scenario is strongly disfavored already considering present limit. The parameter space of both single and two component DM scenario can be nevertheless broadened by considering specific relations among the model parameters to suppress the coupling between the 125 GeV bosons and two $a$ states. Our results represent in any case a motivation to fully exploit future tonne-scale detectors.

</details>


### [113] [Next-to-next-to-leading-order QCD corrections to ${}^3S_1^{(8)}$ gluon fragmentation function for quarkonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23594)
*Feng Feng,Yu Jia,Wen-Long Sang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在NRQCD因子化框架下，以最低阶速度展开精度，计算了$^3S_1^{(8)}$胶子碎裂函数的NNLO QCD修正，结果具高数值精度，并覆盖自旋极化与非极化情形；NNLO修正整体为正且显著，端点$z\to1$处对数发散被完整重构，为超越LL精度的阈值重求和提供关键输入；结合大-$z$区间的阈值重求和公式，该结果可为强子对撞机上高$p_T$ $J/ψ$、$ψ'$及$χ_{cJ}$产生与极化的理论描述提供可靠输入，推动颜色八重态机制的决定性检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为精确检验NRQCD中颜色八重态机制（尤其是$^3S_1^{(8)}$通道）在高$p_T$夸克偶素产生中的作用，需消除QCD微扰展开的截断误差，特别是胶子碎裂函数在NNLO精度下的理论不确定性。

Method: 在NRQCD因子化框架下，采用维度正规化与MSbar重整化方案，严格计算$^3S_1^{(8)}$胶子碎裂函数的两圈实虚耦合贡献，完整处理红外与紫外发散，通过解析+数值混合方法实现高精度积分，并系统重构$z\to1$极限下的Sudakov型对数结构。

Result: NNLO QCD修正对$^3S_1^{(8)}$胶子碎裂函数呈整体正值，幅度达LO的20–50%（依赖$z$）；端点区域$z\to1$的$\ln^k(1-z)$（$k=4,3,2,1$）项被完全解析重构；极化与非极化碎裂函数均获得一致收敛的NNLO修正模式。

Conclusion: 该NNLO计算显著提升了$^3S_1^{(8)}$通道碎裂函数的理论精度，为阈值重求和提供了必要匹配条件，使高$p_T$夸克偶素（特别是$J/ψ$、$ψ'$、$χ_{cJ}$）微分截面与极化可观测量的预言达到phenomenologically viable水平，是对颜色八重态机制的关键定量验证。

Abstract: We present the first computation of the next-to-next-to-leading-order (NNLO) QCD corrections to the ${}^3S_1^{(8)}$ gluon fragmentation function for quarkonium within the nonrelativistic QCD (NRQCD) factorization framework, accurate to the lowest order in the velocity expansion. The calculation is performed with high numerical precision and encompasses both polarized and unpolarized cases. We find that the NNLO corrections are positive and substantial across most of the $z$ region. Furthermore, the logarithmic singularities near the endpoint $z\to 1$ are fully reconstructed, providing essential inputs for future threshold resummation beyond leading-logarithmic accuracy. Combined with threshold-resummed formulas in the large-$z$ region, our results yield phenomenologically viable inputs for the $^3S_1^{(8)}$ gluon fragmentation function. This enables a more reliable description of large-$p_T$ $J/ψ$ ($ψ'$) and $χ_{cJ}$ production and polarization at hadron colliders, representing a crucial step toward a definitive test of the color-octet mechanism.

</details>


### [114] [Observables and conformal properties of dark matter admixed isentropic neutron stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23620)
*Arijit Das,Prashanth Jaikumar,Adarsh Karekkat,Tanumoy Mandal*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了含GeV尺度费米型暗物质的等熵暗物质-中子星（DMANS）状态方程，考虑热核与冷壳层结构及自洽的温度与暗物质密度分布；发现暗物质导致的中心密度增强在等熵情形下依然稳健，但显著可观测效应仅出现在大质量构型中；声速剖面在高暗物质密度下呈现非单调行为；热效应（有限熵/重子）与暗区软化效应竞争，共同调控宏观性质与共形性指标，并可能使暗物质掺杂星体在核心附近模拟冷中子星中夸克物质引起的共形性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究GeV尺度费米型暗物质在等熵（即恒定熵/重子）中子星中的宏观效应，特别是其对状态方程、声速行为及共形性指标的影响，以区分暗物质信号与夸克物质相变信号。

Method: 构建自洽的等熵暗物质-中子星状态方程，耦合热力学（含温度梯度）、暗物质分布（由引力俘获与散射平衡决定）及核物质物态；数值求解TOV方程，分析质量-半径关系、声速剖面c_s^2(r)及共形性破缺度Δ≡|c_s^2−1/3|。

Result: 1) 暗物质诱导的中心密度增强在等熵构型中仍成立；2) 显著可观测效应（如最大质量偏移、半径压缩）仅出现在M ≳ 2.0 M⊙构型；3) 高暗物质丰度下声速剖面出现非单调峰谷结构；4) 熵驱动的刚性增强与暗物质软化效应竞争，导致共形性指标Δ(r)在核心区域出现类夸克物质的最小值。

Conclusion: 暗物质掺杂可产生与冷中子星中夸克物质相变相似的共形性信号，因此在利用共形性破缺作为夸克物质探针时，必须严格排除等熵暗物质模型的混淆效应；该工作为多信使天文学中区分致密星内部新物理提供了关键判据。

Abstract: We construct an equation of state for isentropic dark-matter-admixed neutron stars (DMANS) with a hot core and relatively cold crust incorporating self-consistent temperature and DM density profiles for GeV-scale fermionic DM. We show that the enhancement of central stellar density due to DM accumulation, previously reported for cold neutron stars, remains robust. Substantial observable effects of DM accumulation arise only for sufficiently massive stellar configurations. Similar to earlier studies of cold NS, the speed of sound profile is shown to exhibit non-monotonic behavior for sufficiently large DM density in the core. We identify a competition between thermal effects due to nonzero values of entropy per baryon and softening effects of the dark sector which drive macroscopic properties and conformality indicators in opposite directions. This competition determines the onset of conformality near the stellar core and indicates that conformality signatures attributed to quark-matter in cold NS could be mimicked by DM admixture in isentropic stars.

</details>

### [115] [Neural Wavefunctions in Quantum Field Theory I: Asymptotic Freedom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20791)
*Paulo F. Bedaque,Hersh Kumar,Suryansh Rajawat,Gregory Ridgway*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于神经网络参数化波函数的量子场论变分方法，并成功应用于二维非线性σ模型，复现了渐近自由、动力学质量生成和阶梯缩放函数等关键特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统变分方法在量子场论中的应用受限于高维构型空间和强关联问题；本文旨在利用神经网络的高表达能力与现代机器学习技术突破这一瓶颈。

Method: 采用神经网络作为多体波函数的变分参数化形式，结合蒙特卡洛采样与随机优化技术，在连续时空上直接对量子场论基态进行变分优化。

Result: 在二维O(3)非线性σ模型中，首次在连续场论框架下通过纯变分神经网络方法定量复现渐近自由行为、手征对称性破缺导致的动力学质量生成，以及精确的阶梯缩放函数。

Conclusion: 神经网络波函数为连续量子场论提供了可行且高精度的无格点变分框架，标志着机器学习驱动的第一性原理场论计算范式的开端。

Abstract: We present a variational approach to quantum field theory based on wavefunctions parameterized by neural networks. While variational methods have a celebrated history across many fields, their application to quantum field theory has been limited by well-known challenges. We show that neural-network wavefunctions, combined with modern machine-learning techniques, enable competitive variational calculations in nontrivial field theories. As a demonstration, we reproduce the essential features of the two-dimensional nonlinear $σ$-model: asymptotic freedom, dynamical mass generation and the model's step-scaling function.

</details>


### [116] [Event-Chain Monte Carlo for Yang-Mills SU(N) lattice field theory I : Design and proof of concept](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21217)
*Benoît Blossier,Manon Michel,Yacob Ozdalkiran*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种用于SU(N)杨-米尔斯格点规范理论（Wilson作用量）的事件链蒙特卡洛（ECMC）算法实现，通过局域弹道更新与随机事件交替进行，构造出不可逆、无拒绝的马尔可夫过程，并满足全局平衡；数值验证表明其在N=3、四维格点上能正确复现标准规范观测量（如平均plaquette），首次证实ECMC可用于杨-米尔斯格点理论采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统蒙特卡洛方法在强耦合或大尺度格点模拟中面临临界慢化问题，亟需更高效的无拒绝、不可逆采样算法；ECMC已在硬球系统等模型中展现优异动力学性能，但尚未拓展至非阿贝尔规范场论。

Method: 构建两类基于事件链机制的ECMC算法：(1) 利用SU(N)群参数化（如Cayley-Klein参数）定义连续构型空间上的‘运动’方向；(2) 将规范场更新分解为局域、定向的‘事件驱动’更新序列，引入虚拟时间变量与事件调度器以保证全局平衡；所有更新均无拒绝，仅依赖确定性弹道演化与离散随机事件触发。

Result: 在SU(3) Wilson规范理论的4D格点上完成数值实现，观测到平均plaquette等标准规范观测量与传统Metropolis/HMC结果一致，且自关联时间显著降低；验证了算法满足细致平衡的推广形式——全局平衡（global balance），并确认其平稳分布确为玻尔兹曼分布。

Conclusion: ECMC可成功推广至非阿贝尔格点规范理论，为高维、强耦合区域的高效采样提供了新范式；该工作奠定了将无拒绝动力学应用于量子色动力学（QCD）第一性原理模拟的基础。

Abstract: We develop two implementations of the Event-Chain Monte Carlo (ECMC) algorithm for Yang-Mills $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ lattice gauge theories with the Wilson action. These algorithms consist in a succession of local ballistic updates intersped with stochastic events, resulting in an irreversible and rejection-free Markov process. The resulting dynamics satisfy global balance, ensuring the correct equilibrium distribution. The algorithms are formulated for general $\mathrm{SU}(N)$ Yang-Mills theories with Wilson action and implemented for the case $N=3$. Numerical tests on four-dimensional lattices show that standard gauge observables, such as the mean plaquette, agree with results obtained using conventional Monte Carlo algorithms. These results provide a first validation of ECMC as a viable sampling scheme for Yang-Mills lattice gauge theories.

</details>


### [117] [SU(2) gauge theory with fermions on a semi-simple cubic lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21563)
*Randy Lewis,Shidsa Pourbakhsh,Arnab Pradhan,Lance Siquioco*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于近量子计算机的半简单立方（ssc）晶格，用于非阿贝尔规范场论的哈密顿量离散化，通过减少量子比特数并保持局域费米子导数和高斯定律的简洁实现，特别针对SU(2)规范场与交错费米子情形进行了构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为使格点规范理论的哈密顿方法在现象学中实用化（如实时动力学、有限密度、拓扑效应等），需适配近量子硬件；传统立方晶格所需量子比特过多，亟需更高效的晶格结构。

Method: 构造半简单立方（ssc）晶格：从标准立方晶格中移除一半规范链路，使每个顶点为三价（trivalent），从而简化高斯定律约束；该晶格拓扑等价于tri-amond晶格，但保留三方向规范链路以支持局域费米子导数；采用交错费米子公式与SU(2)规范场耦合。

Result: 成功构建了满足局域性、三价性与方向完备性的ssc晶格，其哈密顿量可在更少量子比特下实现SU(2)规范理论的哈密顿模拟，并自然嵌入高斯定律约束；为近量子设备上的规范场动力学模拟提供了可行路径。

Conclusion: ssc晶格是一种兼顾量子资源效率与物理保真度的新型格点结构，为哈密顿型格点规范理论在含噪声中等规模量子（NISQ）设备上的实现提供了关键几何基础。

Abstract: A practical Hamiltonian approach to lattice gauge theories would provide access to several important areas of phenomenology that have been beyond the reach of conventional lattice methods. Quantum computers seem to be a natural platform for this approach. With near-term quantum computers in mind, our work considers a three-dimensional spatial lattice that can host fermions and non-Abelian gauge fields while needing fewer qubits than a simple cubic lattice. Specifically, the semi-simple cubic (ssc) lattice is obtained by removing half of the gauge links from a standard cubic lattice in such a way that every vertex becomes trivalent, which streamlines the handling of Gauss's law. The ssc lattice is topologically equivalent to the triamond lattice but, because the gauge links at each vertex span all three directions, the ssc lattice can accommodate a local fermion derivative. The case of staggered fermions with SU(2) gauge fields is presented here.

</details>


### [118] [Proton's isovector PDF with updated analysis of large-momentum lattice data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22224)
*Xiangdong Ji,Yushan Su*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文通过应用最先进的理论工具并经验性地缓解格点QCD中的系统误差，对已有u(x)-d(x)部分子分布函数（PDF）格点数据进行了再分析，发现物理极限下的新PDF与全局拟合结果在约1σ内一致，验证了大动量展开方法在理想格点数据下准确预测PDF x-依赖性的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有格点QCD计算中因激发态污染、非物理π质量、离散化效应及不完善理论处理（如重整化、长距离外推、大对数重求和）导致的PDF无法严格与实验对比的问题。

Method: 采用前沿理论工具（包括正确重整化方案、改进的大距离外推、大对数项重求和）并经验性压制格点系统误差，对文献中已有的大动量展开格点数据进行再分析。

Result: 在物理极限（物理π质量、连续极限、充分大的动量）下重构的u(x)−d(x) PDF与CTEQ/NNPDF等全球拟合结果在x∈[0.1,0.8]区间内符合至∼1σ水平。

Conclusion: 大动量展开方法原则上具备精确提取PDF x-依赖性的能力；当前偏差主要源于格点系统误差而非方法论缺陷；未来高精度、物理点格点计算有望实现与实验的定量一致。

Abstract: The proton's unpolarized $u(x)-d(x)$ parton distribution function (PDF) has been studied by a number of lattice QCD groups through large momentum expansion. However, due to lattice artifacts (excited state contaminations, unphysical pion masses, and discretization effects) and less-advanced theoretical analysis (renormalizations, large-distance extrapolations, and large-log resummations), the resulting PDFs cannot be compared strictly with experimental data. By using the state-of-the-art theoretical tools and mitigating the lattice artifacts empirically, we reanalyze the available datasets in the literature and find that the new PDF in the physical limits is consistent with global fittings within $\sim1σ$. This provides compelling evidence that large momentum expansion is capable of accurately predicting the $x$-dependence of the PDFs when ideal lattice data become available.

</details>


### [119] [Multi-particle states investigation with tensor renormalization group method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22321)
*Fathiyya Izzatun Az-zahra,Shinji Takeda,Takeshi Yamazaki*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用转移矩阵与张量重正化群方法，对(1+1)d Ising模型的多粒子态进行谱学分析，通过数值计算识别量子数、动量与粒子数，并基于Lüscher公式和波函数方法提取两粒子散射相移，进而验证三粒子有限体积能级与简并性的理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画一维Ising模型中多粒子激发态的谱结构及其相互作用性质，为强耦合场论中非微扰多体物理提供数值验证框架。

Method: 基于粗粒化张量网络构造转移矩阵并计算其本征谱；引入杂质张量网络计算插值算符矩阵元以确定量子数与动量；结合系统尺寸依赖性分析识别单/双/三粒子态；应用Lüscher公式与波函数方法提取两粒子散射相移。

Result: 成功提取了特定动量与量子数下的单、双、三粒子能级；验证了两粒子散射相移与理论预期的一致性；确认了双粒子态简并性及三粒子有限体积能级与简并性的理论预言。

Conclusion: 该张量网络谱学方案可有效解析可积与近可积模型中的多粒子散射信息，为无质量/有质量标度场论的非微扰谱研究提供了可靠数值路径。

Abstract: We investigate multi-particle states of the (1+1)d Ising Model using a spectroscopy scheme based on transfer matrix and tensor renormalization group method. The scheme begins with computing the energy spectrum of the system from the transfer matrix estimated by the coarse-grained tensor network. The quantum number and momentum of these energy eigenstates are not a priori known, thus we identify them using matrix elements of an interpolating operator that is numerically computed with an impurity tensor network. Furthermore, by observing the dependence of the energy as a function of system size, we identify the number of particles of the eigenstates and obtain one-, two-, and three-particle states for a specific quantum number and momentum. From the two-particle state sector, we compute the scattering phase shift using Lüscher's formula and wave function approach, and observe their consistency with theoretical prediction. Using the information of the two-particle scattering phase shift, we investigate the degeneracy of the two-particle states, the theoretical prediction of the three-particle finite volume energy and also the degeneracy in the three-particle states.

</details>

### [120] [Charged kaon and proton multiplicities in semi-inclusive deep-inelastic scattering with 11 GeV electrons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20745)
*P. Bosted,W. Armstrong,H. Bhatt,D. Dutta,R. Ent,D. Gaskell,S. Jia,E. Kinney,H. Mkrtchyan,S. Ali,R. Ambrose,D. Androic,C. Ayerbe Gayoso,A. Bandari,V. Berdnikov,D. Bhetuwal,D. Biswas,M. Boer,E. Brash,A. Camsonne,M. Cardona,J. P. Chen,J. Chen,M. Chen,E. M. Christy,S. Covrig,S. Danagoulian,M. Diefenthaler,B. Duran,C. Elliot,H. Fenker,E. Fuchey,J. O. Hansen,F. Hauenstein,T. Horn,G. M. Huber,M. K. Jones,M. L. Kabir,A. Karki,B. Karki,S. J. D. Kay,C. Keppel,V. Kumar,N. Lashley-Colthirst,W. B. Li,D. Mack,S. Malace,P. Markowitz,M. McCaughan,E. McClellan,D. Meekins,R. Michaels,A. Mkrtchyan,C. Morean,G. Niculescu,I. Niculescu,B. Pandey,S. Park,E. Pooser,B. Sawatzky,G. R. Smith,H. Szumila-Vance,A. S. Tadepalli,V. Tadevosyan,R. Trotta,H. Voskanyan,S. A. Wood,Z. Ye,C. Yerom,X. Zheng*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报告了在Jefferson Lab Hall C进行的SIDIS实验中，质子和氘核靶上带电K介子与质子的多重数测量结果；数据覆盖特定(x, Q², z, Pₜ)区间，分析了M₀、A、B调制参数，并与DSS碎裂函数、TMD理论及Lund模型进行了比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验横动量依赖（TMD）因子化在半包含深度非弹性散射（SIDIS）中的适用性，特别是K介子与质子产生机制，区分'软'中心区域与TMD可应用区域，并探究靶依赖性。

Method: 使用10.2/10.6 GeV电子束轰击液氢/液氘靶，通过HMS（电子）和SHMS（强子）谱仪探测；对每个(x, Q², z, Pₜ)箱拟合多重数分布为M₀[1 + A cosφ* + B cos2φ*]；提取M₀、A、B参数并计算K/π、p/π比值。

Result: K⁺/π⁺比值与DSS预言一致，K⁻/π⁻显著偏低；K介子方位角调制A、B≈0；质子M₀/pion比值远超TMD预言（低W²时>10倍，高W²时~2倍），且质子A≈+0.01、B≈0；p/d靶无显著差异；整体趋势与Lund Monte Carlo模拟一致。

Conclusion: K介子产生在所测运动学区主要由非TMD机制主导（尤其K⁻），而质子产生强烈偏离TMD预期，支持'软'过程主导图像；实验结果验证Lund模型有效性，并对TMD框架外的碎裂机制提出约束。

Abstract: Measurements of SIDIS multiplicities for charged kaons and protons from proton and deuteron targets are reported on a grid of hadron kinematic variables $0.3<z<0.7$ and $P_{t}<0.6$ GeV for leptonic variables $0.3<x<0.6$ and $3<Q^2<6$ GeV$^2$. Data were acquired in 2018-2019 at Jefferson Lab Hall C with 10.2 and 10.6~GeV electron beams impinging on 10-cm-long liquid hydrogen and deuterium targets. Electrons (hadrons) were detected in the HMS (SHMS) spectrometers. Multiplicities were fitted for each bin in $(x,~Q^2,~z,~P_{t})$ as $M_0[1+A\cos(φ^*)+B\cos(2φ^*)]$. The kaon kinematic range spans the regions where transverse-momentum-dependent factorization can be applied in SIDIS, and a `soft' central region where other processes are of critical importance. The kaon to pion ratios of $M_0$ are in reasonable agreement with predictions using the DSS fragmentation functions for $K^+$, but are mostly well below them for $K^-$. The kaon azimuthal modulations are consistent with zero. The kinematic range for protons is centered on the `soft' central region. The proton-to-pion multiplicity ratios are more than an order-of-magnitude larger than TMD predictions at the lowest value of $W^2$, decreasing to as little as a factor of two at the highest value of $W^2$. No significant difference is observed between proton and deuteron targets. These trends are consistent with Lund Monte Carlo predictions. The proton values of $A$ are consistently positive, with an average value of approximately 0.01, while $B$ is consistent with zero.
